\section{Sub graph layout}\label{sub-graph-layout}

\subsection{bipartite network}\label{bipartite-network}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Extract the subgraph corresponding to module "1" and "7".}
\CommentTok{\# Internally, get\_subgraph():}
\CommentTok{\#   1) splits all nodes by the \textasciigrave{}Modularity\textasciigrave{} attribute,}
\CommentTok{\#   2) builds one subgraph per module and stores them in \textasciigrave{}sub\_graph\_all\textasciigrave{},}
\CommentTok{\#   3) if \textasciigrave{}select\_module\textasciigrave{} is provided, also returns \textasciigrave{}sub\_graph\_select\textasciigrave{}}
\CommentTok{\#      (the graph filtered to those modules via tidygraph::filter),}
\CommentTok{\#   4) returns \textasciigrave{}stat\_module\textasciigrave{}: a table of node counts per module across the full graph.}
\NormalTok{graph\_sub }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_subgraph}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{graph\_obj =}\NormalTok{ graph\_obj,}
                          \AttributeTok{select\_module =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"7"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{                          )}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##    Module Number
## 1       1    416
## 2       7    161
## 3       6    137
## 4       9    121
## 5       4    112
## 6       2    105
## 7       3    104
## 8      11    101
## 9       8     87
## 10     10     80
## 11      5     78
## 12     13     70
## 13     16     52
## 14     15     51
## 15     14     46
## 16 Others    328
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{graph\_sub}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{sub\_graph\_select}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\chapter{Network topology information}\label{network-topology-information}

\begin{quote}
Load R Package
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(tidyverse)   }\CommentTok{\# data manipulation / piping utilities}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggNetView)   }\CommentTok{\# graph builders + info{-}extraction helpers}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{quote}
Example data
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Access built{-}in example datasets in ggNetView}

\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Dataset 1: Relative abundance table of rarefied ASVs/OTUs {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Rows = ASVs/OTUs (features / variables), Columns = samples}
\FunctionTok{data}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"otu\_rare\_relative"}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{dim}\NormalTok{(otu\_rare\_relative)  }\CommentTok{\# Check matrix dimensions: n\_features x n\_samples }
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## [1] 2859   18
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{otu\_rare\_relative[}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{] }\CommentTok{\# Preview first 5 rows x 5 cols to confirm data layout}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
##              KO1        KO2        KO3        KO4        KO5
## ASV_1 0.03306667 0.05453333 0.02013333 0.03613333 0.02686667
## ASV_2 0.05750000 0.03393333 0.06046667 0.05810000 0.07320000
## ASV_3 0.01733333 0.01296667 0.02290000 0.02336667 0.03106667
## ASV_4 0.04266667 0.01093333 0.01416667 0.01933333 0.03346667
## ASV_6 0.02646667 0.01856667 0.02110000 0.02353333 0.03806667
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Dataset 2: Taxonomic annotation table for ASVs/OTUs {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Rows = ASVs/OTUs (row names should match the abundance table above)}
\CommentTok{\# Columns = taxonomic ranks (Kingdom \textasciitilde{} Species, etc.)}
\FunctionTok{data}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"tax\_tab"}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{dim}\NormalTok{(tax\_tab)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## [1] 2859    8
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{tax\_tab[}\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{]}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tibble: 5 x 5
##   OTUID  Kingdom  Phylum          Class          Order            
##   <chr>  <chr>    <chr>           <chr>          <chr>            
## 1 ASV_2  Archaea  Thaumarchaeota  Unassigned     Nitrososphaerales
## 2 ASV_3  Bacteria Verrucomicrobia Spartobacteria Unassigned       
## 3 ASV_31 Bacteria Actinobacteria  Actinobacteria Actinomycetales  
## 4 ASV_27 Archaea  Thaumarchaeota  Unassigned     Nitrososphaerales
## 5 ASV_9  Bacteria Unassigned      Unassigned     Unassigned
\end{verbatim}

\begin{quote}
Build graph object
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Build a co{-}occurrence network from an abundance matrix {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Internal pipeline: transform {-}\textgreater{} correlation {-}\textgreater{} p{-}value adjustment {-}\textgreater{}}
\CommentTok{\# edge filtering {-}\textgreater{} community detection {-}\textgreater{} attach taxonomy.}
\NormalTok{graph\_obj }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{build\_graph\_from\_mat}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{mat              =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative,  }\CommentTok{\# variables x samples numeric matrix}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# input already relative abundance, no extra transform}
  \AttributeTok{method           =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# correlation backend: WGCNA::corAndPvalue}
  \AttributeTok{proc             =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# multiple{-}testing correction}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# |r| cutoff for keeping an edge}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# adjusted p{-}value cutoff}
  \AttributeTok{node\_annotation  =}\NormalTok{ tax\_tab,            }\CommentTok{\# taxonomy joined onto each node by name}
  \AttributeTok{top\_modules      =} \DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,                 }\CommentTok{\# keep top{-}15 modules; rest {-}\textgreater{} "Others"}
  \AttributeTok{seed             =} \DecValTok{1115}                \CommentTok{\# fix RNG for reproducibility}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{graph\_obj   }\CommentTok{\# tbl\_graph with Modularity / Degree / Strength + taxonomy columns}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## # A tbl_graph: 213 nodes and 844 edges
## #
## # An undirected simple graph with 29 components
## #
## # Node Data: 213 x 14 (active)

\section{Get network topology information}\label{get-network-topology-information}

\begin{quote}
when \texttt{mat\ =\ NULL}
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Compute network topology + robustness for a single graph {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# When \textasciigrave{}mat = NULL\textasciigrave{}, the function still computes all *purely topological*}
\CommentTok{\# metrics from the igraph object, but the following fields are returned as NA}
\CommentTok{\# because they require the original abundance matrix:}
\CommentTok{\#}
\CommentTok{\#   * Cohension\_Positive  {-} needs per{-}ASV mean abundance to weight positive r}
\CommentTok{\#   * Cohension\_Negative  {-} needs per{-}ASV mean abundance to weight negative r}
\CommentTok{\#   * Robustness\_weight   {-} abundance{-}weighted node{-}removal bootstrap (needs sp.ra)}
\CommentTok{\#   * Robustness\_unweight {-} tied to the same \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{} branch in the code, so also NA}
\CommentTok{\#   * Stability           {-} mean of the two Robustness\_* fields, so also NA}
\CommentTok{\#   * out$Robustness      {-} the whole 5\%{-}100\% removal curve is NA (mean/sd/se)}
\CommentTok{\#}
\CommentTok{\# All other fields (Node, Edge, Degree, Distance, Diameter, Density,}
\CommentTok{\# Transitivity\_global/local, Betweenness, Betweenness\_edge, Closeness,}
\CommentTok{\# Eigen\_centrality, Modularity, K\_core\_mean/max/min, Network\_efficiency,}
\CommentTok{\# Network\_info.centrality, Vulenrability) are computed normally from \textasciigrave{}graph\_obj\textasciigrave{}.}
\NormalTok{topology\_out }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_network\_topology}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{graph\_obj        =}\NormalTok{ graph\_obj,   }\CommentTok{\# input tbl\_graph (required)}
  \AttributeTok{mat              =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{,        }\CommentTok{\# raw abundance matrix; NULL {-}\textgreater{} skip cohesion + robustness}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,      }\CommentTok{\# pre{-}correlation transform (ignored when mat = NULL)}
  \AttributeTok{method           =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,     }\CommentTok{\# correlation backend used to rebuild network.raw from \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{}}
  \AttributeTok{proc             =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}\NormalTok{,}\CommentTok{\# multiple{-}testing correction for p{-}values (used with \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{})}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,         }\CommentTok{\# |r| cutoff applied when rebuilding network.raw from \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{}}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,        }\CommentTok{\# adjusted p{-}value cutoff applied when rebuilding network.raw}
  \AttributeTok{bootstrap        =} \DecValTok{100}          \CommentTok{\# \# of random ER networks for \textasciigrave{}Random\_nerwork\textasciigrave{} column;}
                                  \CommentTok{\# also \# of bootstrap iterations for robustness (only when mat != NULL)}
\NormalTok{)}

\FunctionTok{class}\NormalTok{(topology\_out)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

## [1] "list"
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]

\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Get network topology information with matrix}\label{get-network-topology-information-with-matrix}

\begin{quote}
When a matrix is passed to \texttt{mat}, all topology properties are computed.
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Compute the FULL set of network topology + robustness metrics {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# When \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{} is provided, the function recomputes the thresholded correlation}
\CommentTok{\# matrix from \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{} (used as \textasciigrave{}network.raw\textasciigrave{}) and ALL topology fields are}
\CommentTok{\# returned with real values {-} including the ones that are NA when mat = NULL:}
\CommentTok{\#   Cohension\_Positive / Cohension\_Negative}
\CommentTok{\#   Robustness\_weight  / Robustness\_unweight}
\CommentTok{\#   Stability}
\CommentTok{\#   out$Robustness     (the 5\%{-}100\% node{-}removal curve)}
\NormalTok{topology\_with\_mat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_network\_topology}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{graph\_obj        =}\NormalTok{ graph\_obj,         }\CommentTok{\# input tbl\_graph (required)}
  \AttributeTok{mat              =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative, }\CommentTok{\# raw abundance matrix used to derive sp.ra and rebuild network.raw}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# pre{-}correlation transform on \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{}; "none" = keep as{-}is}
  \AttributeTok{method           =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# correlation backend: WGCNA::corAndPvalue}
  \AttributeTok{proc             =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}\NormalTok{,      }\CommentTok{\# multiple{-}testing correction}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# |r| cutoff for keeping an edge in network.raw}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# adjusted p{-}value cutoff for keeping an edge}
  \AttributeTok{bootstrap        =} \DecValTok{100}                \CommentTok{\# \# of bootstrap iterations for robustness AND \# of random ER networks for the Random\_nerwork column}
\NormalTok{)}


\NormalTok{topology\_with\_mat}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Get network topology information from sample}\label{get-network-topology-information-from-sample}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Per{-}sample subgraph topology {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Step 1: split \textasciigrave{}graph\_obj\textasciigrave{} into per{-}sample subgraphs by the abundance matrix}
\CommentTok{\#         (an OTU is "present" in a sample when its abundance \textgreater{} min\_abundance).}
\CommentTok{\# Step 2: run the full \textasciigrave{}get\_network\_topology()\textasciigrave{} pipeline on EACH sample subgraph.}
\CommentTok{\# When \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{} is supplied, every topology field (including cohesion, robustness,}
\CommentTok{\# stability) is computed for each sample.}
\NormalTok{t3\_group }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{system.time}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  topology\_from\_sample }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_sample\_subgraph\_topology}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{graph\_obj        =}\NormalTok{ graph\_obj,         }\CommentTok{\# input tbl\_graph (the full network)}
    \AttributeTok{mat              =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative, }\CommentTok{\# abundance matrix used for both presence{-}call AND cohesion/robustness}
    \AttributeTok{transfrom.method =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# pre{-}correlation transform on \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{}; "none" = keep as{-}is}
    \AttributeTok{method           =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# correlation backend: WGCNA::corAndPvalue}
    \AttributeTok{cor.method       =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,         }\CommentTok{\# Pearson correlation}
    \AttributeTok{proc             =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}\NormalTok{,      }\CommentTok{\# multiple{-}testing correction }
    \AttributeTok{bootstrap        =} \DecValTok{100}                \CommentTok{\# \# of bootstrap iterations for robustness; also \# of random ER nets for reference}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{topology\_from\_sample   }\CommentTok{\# one topology table per sample, plus per{-}sample robustness curves}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Get network topology information by parallel}\label{get-network-topology-information-by-parallel}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Same as get\_network\_topology(), but with parallel execution {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Useful when \textasciigrave{}bootstrap\textasciigrave{} is large (the random{-}ER{-}network reference loop is}
\CommentTok{\# the main bottleneck) or when running on \textasciigrave{}graph\_obj\_list\textasciigrave{} of many networks.}
\CommentTok{\# With \textasciigrave{}mat = NULL\textasciigrave{}, cohesion / robustness / stability fields are NA {-} see}
\CommentTok{\# get\_network\_topology() docs for the full list of skipped metrics.}
\NormalTok{t4 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{system.time}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  topology\_parallel }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_network\_topology\_parallel}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{graph\_obj        =}\NormalTok{ graph\_obj,}
    \AttributeTok{mat              =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# no abundance matrix {-}\textgreater{} cohesion + robustness = NA}
    \AttributeTok{transfrom.method =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,         }\CommentTok{\# ignored when mat = NULL}
    \AttributeTok{method           =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,        }\CommentTok{\# ignored when mat = NULL (no correlation rebuild)}
    \AttributeTok{cor.method       =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,      }\CommentTok{\# ignored when mat = NULL}
    \AttributeTok{proc             =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}\NormalTok{,   }\CommentTok{\# ignored when mat = NULL}
    \AttributeTok{bootstrap        =} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# \# of random ER networks for the Random\_nerwork column}
    \AttributeTok{parallel         =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# enable parallel backend}
    \AttributeTok{n\_workers        =} \DecValTok{2}               \CommentTok{\# \# of parallel workers (keep \textless{}= cores; 2 is safe under R CMD check)}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Get network topology information with matrix by parallel}\label{get-network-topology-information-with-matrix-by-parallel}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Parallel + abundance matrix {-}\textgreater{} FULL topology panel {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Same as above, but \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{} is now provided, so every topology field gets a}
\CommentTok{\# real value (cohesion, robustness, stability included).}
\NormalTok{t5 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{system.time}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  topology\_parallel\_with\_mat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_network\_topology\_parallel}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{graph\_obj        =}\NormalTok{ graph\_obj,}
    \AttributeTok{mat              =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative, }\CommentTok{\# abundance matrix {-}\textgreater{} enables cohesion + robustness}
    \AttributeTok{transfrom.method =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# pre{-}correlation transform on \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{}}
    \AttributeTok{method           =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# correlation backend used to rebuild network.raw from \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{}}
    \AttributeTok{cor.method       =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,         }\CommentTok{\# Pearson correlation}
    \AttributeTok{proc             =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}\NormalTok{,      }\CommentTok{\# multiple{-}testing correction}
    \AttributeTok{bootstrap        =} \DecValTok{100}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# bootstrap iterations for robustness + \# of random ER reference nets}
    \AttributeTok{parallel         =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# enable parallel backend}
    \AttributeTok{n\_workers        =} \DecValTok{2}                  \CommentTok{\# \# of parallel workers}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

## 39               0.95  0.01413146 0.009519073 0.0009519073 unweighted
## 40               1.00  0.00000000 0.000000000 0.0000000000 unweighted
\end{verbatim}

\section{Integrated Value of Influence (IVI)}\label{integrated-value-of-influence-ivi}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Compute IVI (Salavaty et al. 2020) per node {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# IVI integrates 6 centrality measures across 3 scales (local / semi{-}local /}
\CommentTok{\# global) into a single robust influence score. Thin wrapper over}
\CommentTok{\# influential::ivi() — that package must be installed.}
\NormalTok{obj\_aug }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_node\_ivi}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{graph\_obj =}\NormalTok{ graph\_obj,   }\CommentTok{\# input tbl\_graph}
  \AttributeTok{weights   =} \ConstantTok{NULL}\NormalTok{,        }\CommentTok{\# NULL = unweighted IVI; pass "weight" to use the graph\textquotesingle{}s \textasciigrave{}weight\textasciigrave{} edge attribute}
  \AttributeTok{mode      =} \StringTok{"all"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# edge mode; "all" is the right choice for undirected graphs}
  \AttributeTok{directed  =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# treat the graph as undirected}
  \AttributeTok{d         =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# search radius for the collective{-}influence component (paper recommends 3)}
  \AttributeTok{scale     =} \StringTok{"z{-}scale"}\NormalTok{,   }\CommentTok{\# "range" [1,100] for single{-}network view, "z{-}scale" for cross{-}network / thresholding (z \textgreater{} 1.645), "none" for raw}
  \AttributeTok{ncores    =} \DecValTok{1}            \CommentTok{\# parallel cores for the cluster{-}rank step; 1 is the safe default}
\NormalTok{)}


\NormalTok{obj\_aug         }\CommentTok{\# tbl\_graph with one extra node column: IVI}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggNetView)   }\CommentTok{\# graph builders + info{-}extraction helpers}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{quote}
Example data
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Access built{-}in example datasets in ggNetView}

\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Dataset 1: Relative abundance table of rarefied ASVs/OTUs {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Rows = ASVs/OTUs (features / variables), Columns = samples}
\FunctionTok{data}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"otu\_rare\_relative"}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{dim}\NormalTok{(otu\_rare\_relative)  }\CommentTok{\# Check matrix dimensions: n\_features x n\_samples }

\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Dataset 2: Taxonomic annotation table for ASVs/OTUs {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Rows = ASVs/OTUs (row names should match the abundance table above)}
\CommentTok{\# Columns = taxonomic ranks (Kingdom \textasciitilde{} Species, etc.)}

\FunctionTok{dim}\NormalTok{(tax\_tab)}

## 2 ASV_3  Bacteria Verrucomicrobia Spartobacteria Unassigned

\end{verbatim}

\begin{quote}
Build graph object
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Build a co{-}occurrence network from an abundance matrix {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Internal pipeline: transform {-}\textgreater{} correlation {-}\textgreater{} p{-}value adjustment {-}\textgreater{}}
\CommentTok{\# edge filtering {-}\textgreater{} community detection {-}\textgreater{} attach taxonomy.}
\NormalTok{graph\_obj }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{build\_graph\_from\_mat}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{mat              =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative,  }\CommentTok{\# variables x samples numeric matrix}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# input already relative abundance, no extra transform}
  \AttributeTok{method           =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# correlation backend: WGCNA::corAndPvalue}
  \AttributeTok{proc             =} \StringTok{"bonferroni"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# multiple{-}testing correction}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# |r| cutoff for keeping an edge}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# adjusted p{-}value cutoff}
  \AttributeTok{node\_annotation  =}\NormalTok{ tax\_tab,            }\CommentTok{\# taxonomy joined onto each node by name}
  \AttributeTok{top\_modules      =} \DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,                 }\CommentTok{\# keep top{-}15 modules; rest {-}\textgreater{} "Others"}
  \AttributeTok{seed             =} \DecValTok{1115}                \CommentTok{\# fix RNG for reproducibility}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{graph\_obj   }\CommentTok{\# tbl\_graph with Modularity / Degree / Strength + taxonomy columns}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{quote}
Extract the two inputs Zi-Pi needs from graph object
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{nodes\_bulk }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_graph\_nodes}\NormalTok{(graph\_obj)      }\CommentTok{\# node table (module + degree cols)}
\NormalTok{adj\_mat    }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{get\_graph\_adjacency}\NormalTok{(graph\_obj)  }\CommentTok{\# adjacency / correlation matrix}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Zi-Pi analysis and plot}\label{zi-pi-analysis-and-plot}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{role\_palette }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
  \StringTok{"Peripherals"}  \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#377eb8"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Connectors"}   \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#4daf4a"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Module hubs"}  \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#e41a1c"}\NormalTok{,}
  \StringTok{"Network hubs"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#ff7f00"}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{zp }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggnetview\_zipi}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{nodes\_bulk     =}\NormalTok{ nodes\_bulk,    }\CommentTok{\# node table: IDs in rownames or a \textasciigrave{}name\textasciigrave{} col}
  \AttributeTok{z\_bulk\_mat     =}\NormalTok{ adj\_mat,       }\CommentTok{\# adjacency matrix; non{-}zero entries = edges}
  \AttributeTok{modularity\_col =} \StringTok{"Modularity"}\NormalTok{,  }\CommentTok{\# column holding module labels (no NAs)}
  \AttributeTok{degree\_col     =} \StringTok{"Degree"}\NormalTok{,      }\CommentTok{\# column holding node degree   (no NAs)}
  \AttributeTok{zi\_threshold   =} \FloatTok{2.5}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# Zi cutoff for role classification}
  \AttributeTok{pi\_threshold   =} \FloatTok{0.62}\NormalTok{,          }\CommentTok{\# Pi cutoff for role classification}
  \AttributeTok{na.rm          =} \ConstantTok{FALSE}\NormalTok{,         }\CommentTok{\# FALSE = keep unclassifiable nodes (type = NA)}
  \AttributeTok{point\_colors   =}\NormalTok{ role\_palette,  }\CommentTok{\# point + legend colours, keyed by role}
  \AttributeTok{bg\_colors      =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(             }\CommentTok{\# quadrant background fills, keyed by role}
    \StringTok{"Peripherals"}  \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#f0f0f0"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Connectors"}   \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#d9f0d3"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Module hubs"}  \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#fde0dd"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"Network hubs"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#fee8c8"}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{label\_colors   =}\NormalTok{ role\_palette,  }\CommentTok{\# quadrant text{-}label colours (default: black)}
  \AttributeTok{label\_size     =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# quadrant text{-}label size  (default 5.5)}
  \AttributeTok{bg\_alpha       =} \FloatTok{0.35}           \CommentTok{\# background opacity 0..1    (default 0.25)}
\NormalTok{)}


\FunctionTok{class}\NormalTok{(zp)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

Minimal call: only the required arguments

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\#  Minimal call: only the required arguments}

\NormalTok{zp\_min }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggnetview\_zipi}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  nodes\_bulk, adj\_mat,}
  \AttributeTok{modularity\_col =} \StringTok{"Modularity"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{degree\_col     =} \StringTok{"Degree"}
\NormalTok{)}


\CommentTok{\# The three point\_colors styles}
\NormalTok{zp\_unnamed }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggnetview\_zipi}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  nodes\_bulk, adj\_mat, }\StringTok{"Modularity"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Degree"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{point\_colors =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#1b9e77"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#d95f02"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#7570b3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#e7298a"}\NormalTok{)  }\CommentTok{\# by fixed order}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{zp\_named }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggnetview\_zipi}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  nodes\_bulk, adj\_mat, }\StringTok{"Modularity"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Degree"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{point\_colors =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Network hubs"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"purple"}\NormalTok{,                  }\CommentTok{\# override a subset;}
                   \StringTok{"Module hubs"}  \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"firebrick"}\NormalTok{)               }\CommentTok{\# others stay default}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{zp\_single }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggnetview\_zipi}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  nodes\_bulk, adj\_mat, }\StringTok{"Modularity"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Degree"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{point\_colors =} \StringTok{"steelblue"}                                   \CommentTok{\# all four roles}
\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Background tweaks}

\NormalTok{zp\_nobg }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggnetview\_zipi}\NormalTok{(}
\NormalTok{  nodes\_bulk, adj\_mat, }\StringTok{"Modularity"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Degree"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{bg\_alpha =} \DecValTok{0}                       \CommentTok{\# disable quadrant shading entirely}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Comparison of multi-sample networks}\label{comparison-of-multi-sample-networks}

\subsection{layout = ``gephi'', scale = FALSE}\label{layout-gephi-scale-false}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Multi{-}network "linked" view (no per{-}group scale normalisation) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciigrave{}ggNetView\_multi\_link()\textasciigrave{} builds ONE network per group (defined by \textasciigrave{}group\_info\textasciigrave{}),}
\CommentTok{\# lays each one out independently, and places all sub{-}networks on a circular}
\CommentTok{\# anchor frame so that cross{-}group edges/relationships can be visually compared.}
\NormalTok{out1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView\_multi\_link}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{mat              =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative, }\CommentTok{\# variables x samples abundance matrix}
  \AttributeTok{group\_info       =}\NormalTok{ otu\_sample,        }\CommentTok{\# sample metadata; defines how samples are split into groups}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# pre{-}correlation transform on \textasciigrave{}mat\textasciigrave{}; "none" = keep as{-}is}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# |r| cutoff for keeping an edge}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# adjusted p{-}value cutoff}
  \AttributeTok{method           =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# correlation backend: WGCNA::corAndPvalue}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method       =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,         }\CommentTok{\# Pearson correlation}
  \AttributeTok{proc             =} \StringTok{"BH"}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# multiple{-}testing correction (Benjamini{-}Hochberg)}
  \AttributeTok{module.method    =} \StringTok{"Fast\_greedy"}\NormalTok{,     }\CommentTok{\# community detection per sub{-}network}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module    =} \StringTok{"adjacent"}\NormalTok{,        }\CommentTok{\# neighbouring modules placed close together}
  \AttributeTok{center           =}\NormalTok{ T,                 }\CommentTok{\# pull a node toward the centre of each sub{-}layout}
  \AttributeTok{top\_modules      =} \DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# keep top{-}15 modules per sub{-}network; rest {-}\textgreater{} "Others"}
  \AttributeTok{layout           =} \StringTok{"gephi"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# global node{-}positioning algorithm per sub{-}network}
  \AttributeTok{shrink           =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# shrink each sub{-}network inward (smaller = more compact)}
  \AttributeTok{scale            =}\NormalTok{ F,                 }\CommentTok{\# do NOT normalise each group to a common scale {-}\textgreater{} sub{-}networks keep their native size differences}
  \AttributeTok{jitter           =}\NormalTok{ T,                 }\CommentTok{\# jitter node positions slightly to reduce overlap}
  \AttributeTok{jitter\_sd        =} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# SD of the jitter (bigger {-}\textgreater{} more spread)}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist      =} \DecValTok{30}\NormalTok{,                 }\CommentTok{\# distance between groups on the outer anchor circle}
  \AttributeTok{seed             =} \DecValTok{1115}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# fix RNG for reproducibility}
  \AttributeTok{orientation      =} \StringTok{"up"}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# frame orientation; one of "up"/"down"/"left"/"right"}
  \AttributeTok{angle            =} \DecValTok{0}                  \CommentTok{\# additional rotation angle (degrees)}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{layout = ``gephi'', scale = TRUE}\label{layout-gephi-scale-true}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\DocumentationTok{\#\#\#\#{-}{-}{-}{-}Plot2{-}{-}{-}{-}\#\#\#\#}
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Same setup but with \textasciigrave{}scale = TRUE\textasciigrave{} {-}\textgreater{} each group normalised to a common size {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Useful when groups have very different network sizes and you want them}
\CommentTok{\# visually comparable side{-}by{-}side. With \textasciigrave{}scale = TRUE\textasciigrave{} the native size}
\CommentTok{\# differences are removed, so jitter\_sd is also reduced (0.01) accordingly.}
\NormalTok{out2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView\_multi\_link}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{mat              =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative,}
  \AttributeTok{group\_info       =}\NormalTok{ otu\_sample,}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{method           =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method       =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{proc             =} \StringTok{"BH"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{module.method    =} \StringTok{"Fast\_greedy"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module    =} \StringTok{"adjacent"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{center           =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{top\_modules      =} \DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout           =} \StringTok{"gephi"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{shrink           =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{scale            =}\NormalTok{ T,                 }\CommentTok{\# normalise each group to a comparable coordinate scale}
  \AttributeTok{jitter           =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{jitter\_sd        =} \FloatTok{0.01}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# smaller jitter — sub{-}networks are already on a unified scale}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist      =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{seed             =} \DecValTok{1115}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{orientation      =} \StringTok{"up"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{angle            =} \DecValTok{0}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{layout = ``circular\_modules\_equal\_gephi\_layout''}\label{layout-circular_modules_equal_gephi_layout}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Circular{-}modules layout: nodes within each module on equal arcs {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Better than "gephi" when you want module structure to dominate the picture.}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciigrave{}link\_level = "Node"\textasciigrave{}: cross{-}group links are drawn between individual NODES.}
\NormalTok{out\_multi\_link }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView\_multi\_link}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{mat                 =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative,}
  \AttributeTok{group\_info          =}\NormalTok{ otu\_sample,}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method    =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold         =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold         =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{method              =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method          =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{proc                =} \StringTok{"BH"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{module.method       =} \StringTok{"Fast\_greedy"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout              =} \StringTok{"circular\_modules\_equal\_gephi\_layout"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# per{-}sub{-}network layout}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module       =} \StringTok{"order"}\NormalTok{,        }\CommentTok{\# modules placed in a fixed order (not random/adjacent)}
  \AttributeTok{top\_modules         =} \DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_groups        =}\NormalTok{ T,              }\CommentTok{\# normalise each group to a comparable scale}
  \AttributeTok{add\_outer           =} \StringTok{"circle"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# outer frame style; "circle" / "manual" / etc.}
  \AttributeTok{r                   =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# radius of each per{-}group sub{-}network}
  \AttributeTok{jitter              =}\NormalTok{ F,              }\CommentTok{\# don\textquotesingle{}t jitter nodes}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist         =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# distance between groups on the anchor frame}
  \AttributeTok{layout\_anchor\_dist  =} \DecValTok{150}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# anchor frame size in the per{-}sub{-}network layout}
  \AttributeTok{seed                =} \DecValTok{1115}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{dropOthers          =}\NormalTok{ T,              }\CommentTok{\# drop the "Others" module before plotting}
  \AttributeTok{link\_level          =} \StringTok{"Node"}          \CommentTok{\# connect cross{-}group items at NODE level}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Cross{-}group links at "Module \& Node2" level {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Instead of drawing one line per shared node, draw bundled links between}

\NormalTok{out\_multi\_link2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView\_multi\_link}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{mat                 =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative,}
  \AttributeTok{group\_info          =}\NormalTok{ otu\_sample,}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method    =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold         =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold         =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{method              =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method          =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{proc                =} \StringTok{"BH"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{module.method       =} \StringTok{"Fast\_greedy"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout              =} \StringTok{"circular\_modules\_equal\_gephi\_layout"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module       =} \StringTok{"order"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{top\_modules         =} \DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_groups        =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{add\_outer           =} \StringTok{"circle"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{r                   =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{jitter              =}\NormalTok{ F,}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist         =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout\_anchor\_dist  =} \DecValTok{150}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{seed                =} \DecValTok{1115}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{dropOthers          =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{link\_level          =} \StringTok{"Module\&Node2"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# bundled module{-}level + node{-}level cross{-}group links}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer     =}\NormalTok{ T               }\CommentTok{\# draw a translucent boundary around each group}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{add group compare}\label{add-group-compare}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Add cross{-}group comparison brackets + heavy styling {-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# This example shows almost every styling knob the function exposes:}
\CommentTok{\# curved cross{-}group links, group{-}comparison annotations, per{-}group ring}
\CommentTok{\# colours, custom module/node link palettes, etc.}
\NormalTok{out\_multi\_link3 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView\_multi\_link}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{mat                          =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative,}
  \AttributeTok{group\_info                   =}\NormalTok{ otu\_sample,}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method             =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold                  =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold                  =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{method                       =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method                   =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{proc                         =} \StringTok{"BH"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{module.method                =} \StringTok{"Fast\_greedy"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout                       =} \StringTok{"circular\_modules\_equal\_gephi\_layout"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module                =} \StringTok{"order"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{top\_modules                  =} \DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_groups                 =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{add\_outer                    =} \StringTok{"circle"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{r                            =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{jitter                       =}\NormalTok{ F,}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist                  =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout\_anchor\_dist           =} \DecValTok{150}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{seed                         =} \DecValTok{1115}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{dropOthers                   =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{link\_level                   =} \StringTok{"Module\&Node2"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{link\_curve                   =}\NormalTok{ T,             }\CommentTok{\# draw cross{-}group links as curves (not straight)}
  \AttributeTok{link\_curve\_mode              =} \StringTok{"cross"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# curve geometry: "cross" bends links across the centre}
  \AttributeTok{link\_curve\_adaptive\_range    =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{1.5}\NormalTok{),   }\CommentTok{\# min/max curvature; adapts to link length}
  \AttributeTok{link\_curve\_adaptive\_bins     =} \DecValTok{7}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# \# of bins used to map link length {-}\textgreater{} curvature}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons                  =}\NormalTok{ T,             }\CommentTok{\# draw group{-}comparison brackets (like ggpubr style)}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons\_groups           =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(          }\CommentTok{\# which group pairs to highlight}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"WT"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"KO"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"WT"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"OE"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{order                        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"WT"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"KO"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"OE"}\NormalTok{),   }\CommentTok{\# MUST contain ALL unique groups in \textasciigrave{}group\_info$Group\textasciigrave{}}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer              =}\NormalTok{ T,             }\CommentTok{\# draw a coloured ring around each group}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_color        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"steelblue"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"coral"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"forestgreen"}\NormalTok{),       }\CommentTok{\# ring border colours (one per group)}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_fill         =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#d0d1e6"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fee391"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#e5f5e0"}\NormalTok{),           }\CommentTok{\# ring fill colours (one per group)}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_fill\_alpha   =} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# ring fill transparency}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_linewidth    =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# ring border line width}
  \AttributeTok{link\_color\_module            =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#de2d26"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#756bb1"}\NormalTok{),   }\CommentTok{\# palette for MODULE{-}level cross{-}group links}
  \AttributeTok{link\_color\_node              =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#c7e9c0"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#c6dbef"}\NormalTok{),   }\CommentTok{\# palette for NODE{-}level cross{-}group links}
  \AttributeTok{link\_linewidth\_module        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\FloatTok{1.5}\NormalTok{, }\FloatTok{1.5}\NormalTok{),   }\CommentTok{\# line widths for module{-}level links}
  \AttributeTok{link\_linealpha\_module        =} \FloatTok{0.6}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# alpha for module{-}level links}
  \AttributeTok{link\_linealpha\_node          =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# alpha for node{-}level links}
  \AttributeTok{link\_linewidth\_node          =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# line width for node{-}level links}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_expand       =} \FloatTok{2.5}            \CommentTok{\# how much the outer ring is expanded outward beyond node positions}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}

\begin{Shaded}

\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Place the 8 sub{-}networks on a downward{-}pointing TRIANGLE frame {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciigrave{}group\_layout = "triangle\_down"\textasciigrave{} arranges groups along a triangular outline}
\CommentTok{\# with the apex pointing down. Useful for emphasising hierarchy or a "funnel"}
\CommentTok{\# narrative across groups.}
\NormalTok{out\_group4\_1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView\_multi\_link}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{mat                          =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative3,}
  \AttributeTok{group\_info                   =}\NormalTok{ otu\_sample3,}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method             =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold                  =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold                  =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{method                       =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method                   =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{proc                         =} \StringTok{"BH"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{module.method                =} \StringTok{"Fast\_greedy"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module                =} \StringTok{"random"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{center                       =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{top\_modules                  =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout                       =} \StringTok{"gephi"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{group\_layout                 =} \StringTok{"triangle\_down"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{nrow                         =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{shrink                       =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_groups                 =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{add\_outer                    =} \StringTok{"manual"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{r                            =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{jitter                       =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{jitter\_sd                    =} \FloatTok{0.01}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist                  =} \DecValTok{2}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout\_anchor\_dist           =} \DecValTok{250}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{seed                         =} \DecValTok{1115}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{orientation                  =} \StringTok{"up"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{dropOthers                   =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{angle                        =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{node\_add                     =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{pointsize                    =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
  \AttributeTok{link\_level                   =} \StringTok{"Module\&Node2"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons                  =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons\_groups           =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"WT"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"wt"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"wt"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"KO"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"KO"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"ko"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"ko"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"OE"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"OE"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"oe"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"oe"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"aaa"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"aaa"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"bbb"}\NormalTok{)),}
  \AttributeTok{order                        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"WT"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"wt"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"KO"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"ko"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"OE"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"oe"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"aaa"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"bbb"}\NormalTok{), }\CommentTok{\# all 8 groups in display order}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer              =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_fill\_alpha   =} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_linewidth    =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{link\_linealpha\_module        =} \FloatTok{0.6}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{link\_linewidth\_node          =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_expand       =} \FloatTok{2.5}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\subsection{group\_layout = ``snake\_vertical''}\label{group_layout-snake_vertical}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Place sub{-}networks in a vertical SNAKE (zig{-}zag) frame {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciigrave{}group\_layout = "snake\_vertical"\textasciigrave{} runs groups down{-}up{-}down... along a vertical}
\CommentTok{\# meander. Useful when you have many groups and want them to fit a tall canvas.}
\NormalTok{out\_group4\_1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView\_multi\_link}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{mat                          =}\NormalTok{ otu\_rare\_relative3,   }\CommentTok{\# }\AlertTok{NOTE}\CommentTok{: this is the 4{-}group dataset}
  \AttributeTok{group\_info                   =}\NormalTok{ otu\_sample3,          }\CommentTok{\# }\AlertTok{NOTE}\CommentTok{: this is the 4{-}group metadata}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method             =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold                  =} \FloatTok{0.7}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold                  =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{method                       =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method                   =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{proc                         =} \StringTok{"BH"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{module.method                =} \StringTok{"Fast\_greedy"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module                =} \StringTok{"random"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{center                       =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{top\_modules                  =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout                       =} \StringTok{"gephi"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{group\_layout                 =} \StringTok{"snake\_vertical"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{nrow                         =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{shrink                       =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_groups                 =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{add\_outer                    =} \StringTok{"manual"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{r                            =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{jitter                       =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{jitter\_sd                    =} \FloatTok{0.01}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist                  =} \FloatTok{1.25}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{layout\_anchor\_dist           =} \DecValTok{175}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{seed                         =} \DecValTok{1115}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{orientation                  =} \StringTok{"up"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{dropOthers                   =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{angle                        =} \DecValTok{0}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{node\_add                     =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{pointsize                    =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{),}
  \AttributeTok{link\_level                   =} \StringTok{"Module\&Node2"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons                  =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons\_groups           =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"WT"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"wt"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"wt"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"KO"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"KO"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"ko"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"ko"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"OE"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"OE"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"oe"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"oe"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"aaa"}\NormalTok{),}
                                      \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"aaa"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"bbb"}\NormalTok{)),}
  \AttributeTok{order                        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"WT"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"wt"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"KO"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"ko"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"OE"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"oe"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"aaa"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"bbb"}\NormalTok{), }\CommentTok{\# 8 names, but data only has 4 groups {-}\textgreater{} will error}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer              =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_fill\_alpha   =} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_linewidth    =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{link\_linealpha\_module        =} \FloatTok{0.6}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{link\_linewidth\_node          =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer\_expand       =} \FloatTok{2.5}

\chapter{Network \& Environment}\label{network-environment}

\begin{quote}
Load R Package
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(tidyverse)   }\CommentTok{\# data manipulation / piping utilities}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggNetView)   }\CommentTok{\# graph builders + info{-}extraction helpers}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{quote}
Example data
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Built{-}in example datasets shipped with ggNetView:}
\CommentTok{\#   \textasciigrave{}Envdf\_4st\textasciigrave{} {-} environmental table (samples x env variables)}
\CommentTok{\#   \textasciigrave{}Spedf\textasciigrave{}     {-} species/feature table (samples x species)}
\CommentTok{\# Row order MUST match between the two tables.}
\FunctionTok{data}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Envdf\_4st"}\NormalTok{)}
\FunctionTok{data}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Spedf"}\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Network Environment (one core with 4 Envrionment matrix)}\label{network-environment-one-core-with-4-envrionment-matrix}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Plot: 1 central species block + 4 environmental heatmap quadrants {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciigrave{}relation\_method = "correlation"\textasciigrave{} means spec{-}env links are based on}
\CommentTok{\# correlations (psych::corr.test) rather than community distance / mantel.}
\NormalTok{out1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gglink\_heatmaps}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{env                =}\NormalTok{ Envdf\_4st,           }\CommentTok{\# environmental table: rows = samples, cols = env factors}
  \AttributeTok{spec               =}\NormalTok{ Spedf,               }\CommentTok{\# species table: rows = samples (same order as env), cols = species}
  \AttributeTok{env\_select         =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(                }\CommentTok{\# one heatmap quadrant per env block (length must match \textasciigrave{}orientation\textasciigrave{})}
    \AttributeTok{Env01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{14}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env02 =} \DecValTok{15}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{28}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env03 =} \DecValTok{29}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{42}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env04 =} \DecValTok{43}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{56}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_select        =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(                }\CommentTok{\# one central species network per block}
    \AttributeTok{Spec01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{8}                            \CommentTok{\#   take species columns 1..8}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{relation\_method    =} \StringTok{"correlation"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# spec{-}env link rule: "correlation" (vs "mantel" / "vegdist")}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_layout        =} \StringTok{"circle\_outline"}\NormalTok{,    }\CommentTok{\# shape of the central network: nodes on a circle outline}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method         =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# correlation method used for env{-}env tiles and spec{-}env links}
  \AttributeTok{cor.use            =} \StringTok{"pairwise"}\NormalTok{,          }\CommentTok{\# NA handling: use pairwise{-}complete observations}
  \AttributeTok{r                  =} \DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# effective radius of the central network}
  \AttributeTok{distance           =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# offset between the central network and each surrounding heatmap}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelOrient =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,                  }\CommentTok{\# rotate heatmap row/column labels 45 deg to avoid overlap}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelSize   =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# smaller label font (3) since many labels are crammed at 45 deg}
  \AttributeTok{orientation        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(                   }\CommentTok{\# which quadrant each env block lands in (paired with \textasciigrave{}env\_select\textasciigrave{} in order)}
    \StringTok{"top\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"bottom\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"top\_left"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"bottom\_left"}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## The max module in network is 2 we use the 2  modules for next analysis
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{class}\NormalTok{(out1)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}

\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{length}\NormalTok{(out1)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## [1] 3
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{out1[[}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{]]   }\CommentTok{\# gglink\_heatmaps() returns a list of ggplot variants; [[1]] = the default view}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Network Environment (one core with 3 Environment matrix)}\label{network-environment-one-core-with-3-environment-matrix}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Plot: 1 central species block + 3 environmental heatmap quadrants {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# Like the 4{-}quadrant version, but with Env02 dropped and custom styling:}
\CommentTok{\#   {-} drop\_nonsig = TRUE     : hide non{-}significant spec{-}env links}
\CommentTok{\#   {-} HeatmapColorBar        : custom low/high colour pair per quadrant}
\CommentTok{\#   {-} HeatmapPointFill / CorePointFill : override default fill colours}
\NormalTok{out2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gglink\_heatmaps}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{env                =}\NormalTok{ Envdf\_4st,           }\CommentTok{\# environmental table: rows = samples, cols = env factors}
  \AttributeTok{spec               =}\NormalTok{ Spedf,               }\CommentTok{\# species table: rows = samples (same order as env), cols = species}
  \AttributeTok{env\_select         =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(                }\CommentTok{\# 3 env blocks {-}\textgreater{} 3 heatmap quadrants}
    \AttributeTok{Env01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{14}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env03 =} \DecValTok{29}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{40}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env04 =} \DecValTok{43}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{50}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_select        =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Spec01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# one central species network (species cols 1..8)}
  \AttributeTok{relation\_method    =} \StringTok{"correlation"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# spec{-}env link rule: correlation (vs "mantel" / "vegdist")}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_layout        =} \StringTok{"circle\_outline"}\NormalTok{,    }\CommentTok{\# shape of the central network: nodes on a circle outline}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method         =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# correlation method for env{-}env tiles and spec{-}env links}
  \AttributeTok{cor.use            =} \StringTok{"pairwise"}\NormalTok{,          }\CommentTok{\# NA handling: pairwise{-}complete observations}
  \AttributeTok{drop\_nonsig        =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# remove non{-}significant spec{-}env links from the plot (still kept in stats)}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapColorBar    =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(                }\CommentTok{\# one low/high colour pair per env block (length = 3)}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 1: blue {-}\textgreater{} red}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 2: green {-}\textgreater{} purple}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#4393c3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#d6604d"}\NormalTok{)   }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 3: light blue {-}\textgreater{} brick}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointFill   =} \StringTok{"\#8c6bb1"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# fill of the diagonal anchor points on each heatmap}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointFill      =} \StringTok{"\#225ea8"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# fill of the central species nodes}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelOrient =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,                  }\CommentTok{\# rotate heatmap labels 45 deg to avoid overlap}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelSize   =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# smaller label font (3) since many labels are crammed at 45 deg}
  \AttributeTok{r                  =} \DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# effective radius of the central network}
  \AttributeTok{distance           =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# offset between the central network and each surrounding heatmap}
  \AttributeTok{orientation        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(                   }\CommentTok{\# 3 quadrants — length MUST match \textasciigrave{}env\_select\textasciigrave{}}
    \StringTok{"top\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"top\_left"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"bottom\_left"}
\NormalTok{  )}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\section{Network Environment (one core with 4 Envrionment matrix)}\label{network-environment-one-core-with-4-envrionment-matrix-1}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Plot: 1 central species block + 4 environmental heatmap quadrants {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# \textasciigrave{}gglink\_heatmaps()\textasciigrave{} puts a central species network in the middle, surrounds}
\CommentTok{\# it with one env{-}env heatmap per "quadrant", and draws spec{-}env correlation}
\CommentTok{\# links between the central nodes and the diagonal anchor points on each heatmap.}
\NormalTok{p0 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gglink\_heatmaps}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{env                =}\NormalTok{ Envdf\_4st,        }\CommentTok{\# environmental table: rows = samples, cols = env factors}
  \AttributeTok{spec               =}\NormalTok{ Spedf,            }\CommentTok{\# species table: rows = samples (same order as env), cols = species}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_select        =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(             }\CommentTok{\# one central species network per block}
    \AttributeTok{Spec01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{15}                        \CommentTok{\#   take species columns 1..15}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{env\_select         =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(             }\CommentTok{\# one heatmap quadrant per env block (length must match \textasciigrave{}orientation\textasciigrave{})}
    \AttributeTok{Env01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{14}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env02 =} \DecValTok{15}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{28}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env03 =} \DecValTok{29}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{42}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env04 =} \DecValTok{43}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{56}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{orientation        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(                }\CommentTok{\# where each env block is drawn relative to the centre}
    \StringTok{"top\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"bottom\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"top\_left"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"bottom\_left"}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method         =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,        }\CommentTok{\# correlation method (Pearson / Spearman / Kendall)}
  \AttributeTok{cor.use            =} \StringTok{"pairwise"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# NA handling: pairwise{-}complete observations}
  \AttributeTok{distance           =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# offset between central network and the heatmaps (bigger = pushed out further)}
  \AttributeTok{drop\_nonsig        =}\NormalTok{ T,                }\CommentTok{\# hide non{-}significant spec{-}env links (still kept in the stats data)}
  \AttributeTok{r                  =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# effective radius of the central network}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_networks     =}\NormalTok{ T,                }\CommentTok{\# normalise each central network to the same visual radius}
  \AttributeTok{group\_layout       =} \StringTok{"snake"}\NormalTok{,          }\CommentTok{\# anchor arrangement for multi{-}block centres (here only 1 block, so cosmetic)}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_layout        =} \StringTok{"square\_outline"}\NormalTok{,    }\CommentTok{\# shape of the central network: nodes on a circle outline}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist        =} \DecValTok{13}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# spacing between anchors}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelSize   =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# font size of row/column labels on heatmaps}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapSigSize     =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# font size of significance marks (*, **, ***) inside heatmap tiles}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapColorBar    =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(             }\CommentTok{\# one low/high colour pair per heatmap quadrant (length = \# of env blocks)}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 1: blue {-}\textgreater{} red}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 2: green {-}\textgreater{} purple}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#4393c3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#d6604d"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 3: light blue {-}\textgreater{} brick}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#92c5de"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#f4a582"}\NormalTok{)   }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 4: pale blue {-}\textgreater{} peach}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelOrient =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# rotation angle (deg) for heatmap labels — 45 avoids overlap}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineWidth       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{),          }\CommentTok{\# min/max line width for SIGNIFICANT spec{-}env links (mapped by strength)}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineColor       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#9ecae1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# colour gradient endpoints for significant links (low {-}\textgreater{} high)}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointSize   =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# size of the diagonal anchor points where links land on each heatmap}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointSize      =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# size of the central species nodes}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointFill   =} \StringTok{"\#3690c0"}\NormalTok{,        }\CommentTok{\# fill colour of the heatmap diagonal anchor points}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointFill      =} \StringTok{"\#e7298a"}         \CommentTok{\# fill colour of the central species nodes}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\NormalTok{  ),}

    \AttributeTok{Env03 =} \DecValTok{29}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{42}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env04 =} \DecValTok{43}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{56}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_layout        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"circle\_outline"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"square\_outline"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# one layout shape per spec block}
  \AttributeTok{orientation        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{"top\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"bottom\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"top\_left"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"bottom\_left"}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method         =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{cor.use            =} \StringTok{"pairwise"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{distance           =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{drop\_nonsig        =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{r                  =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_networks     =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{group\_layout       =} \StringTok{"snake"}\NormalTok{,          }\CommentTok{\# anchor arrangement for the 2 central networks}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist        =} \DecValTok{13}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelSize   =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapSigSize     =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapColorBar    =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#4393c3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#d6604d"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#92c5de"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#f4a582"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelOrient =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineWidth       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{),}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineColor       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#9ecae1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{),}

\section{Network Environment (three core)}\label{network-environment-three-core}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Plot 2: THREE central species blocks + restricted spec{-}env comparisons {-}{-}{-}{-}}
\CommentTok{\# {-} 3 spec blocks, each with a different layout shape.}
\CommentTok{\# {-} \textasciigrave{}comparisons\_groups\textasciigrave{} restricts which (env\_block, spec\_block) pairs get linked}
\CommentTok{\#   instead of drawing every possible combination.}
\CommentTok{\# {-} \textasciigrave{}scale\_networks = FALSE\textasciigrave{} lets the 3 blocks keep their native sizes.}
\NormalTok{p2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gglink\_heatmaps}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{env                =}\NormalTok{ Envdf\_4st,}
  \AttributeTok{spec               =}\NormalTok{ Spedf,}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_select        =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{Spec01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Spec02 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Spec03 =} \DecValTok{16}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}                       \CommentTok{\# smaller 3rd block to demo size differences}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{env\_select         =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \AttributeTok{Env01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{14}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env02 =} \DecValTok{15}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{28}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env03 =} \DecValTok{29}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{42}\NormalTok{,}
    \AttributeTok{Env04 =} \DecValTok{43}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{56}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{orientation        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}
    \StringTok{"top\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"bottom\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"top\_left"}\NormalTok{,}
    \StringTok{"bottom\_left"}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_layout        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"rectangle\_outline"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"square\_outline"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"circle\_outline"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# one shape per spec block}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method         =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{cor.use            =} \StringTok{"pairwise"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{distance           =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# bigger gap between centre and heatmaps}
  \AttributeTok{drop\_nonsig        =}\NormalTok{ T,}
  \AttributeTok{r                  =} \FloatTok{1.2}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# minimum block radius (because scale\_networks = F, this is the floor)}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_networks     =}\NormalTok{ F,                }\CommentTok{\# do NOT normalise sizes {-}\textgreater{} blocks keep their natural scale}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist        =} \DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelSize   =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapSigSize     =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapColorBar    =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#4393c3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#d6604d"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#92c5de"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#f4a582"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelOrient =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineWidth       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{),}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineColor       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#9ecae1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{),}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons        =}\NormalTok{ T,                }\CommentTok{\# master switch: enable spec{-}env analysis + linking}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons\_groups =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(             }\CommentTok{\# only draw links for these specific (env\_block, spec\_block) pairs}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env01"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec01"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env02"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec02"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env03"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec03"}\NormalTok{),}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env04"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec03"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointSize   =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointSize      =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointFill   =} \StringTok{"\#3690c0"}\NormalTok{,}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointFill      =} \StringTok{"\#e7298a"}
\NormalTok{)}
