\section{Mantel test and drop\_nonsig = T}\label{mantel-test-and-drop_nonsig-t}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{p2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gglink\_heatmaps}\NormalTok{(}
  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Data input {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{env  =}\NormalTok{ Envdf\_4st,                          }\CommentTok{\# environmental data frame: one column per env variable}
  \AttributeTok{spec =}\NormalTok{ Spedf,                              }\CommentTok{\# species data frame: one column per species / taxonomic unit}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Spec blocks (central networks) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Each list element defines one central species network (column indices into \textasciigrave{}spec\textasciigrave{}).}
  \CommentTok{\# The block names (Spec01, Spec02 ...) are used later to match \textasciigrave{}comparisons\_groups\textasciigrave{}.}
  \CommentTok{\# }\AlertTok{NOTE}\CommentTok{: spec\_collapse = TRUE is enabled below, so these 10 blocks are NOT drawn as}
  \CommentTok{\#       networks — each one is collapsed into a single labelled point bearing the block name.}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_select =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Spec01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,}
                     \AttributeTok{Spec02 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,}
                     \AttributeTok{Spec03 =} \DecValTok{16}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                     \AttributeTok{Spec04 =} \DecValTok{16}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                     \AttributeTok{Spec05 =} \DecValTok{16}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                     \AttributeTok{Spec06 =} \DecValTok{16}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                     \AttributeTok{Spec07 =} \DecValTok{5}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                     \AttributeTok{Spec08 =} \DecValTok{21}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{25}\NormalTok{,}
                     \AttributeTok{Spec09 =} \DecValTok{26}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{35}\NormalTok{,}
                     \AttributeTok{Spec10 =} \DecValTok{36}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{44}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Collapse each spec\_block into a single point {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# T = each spec\_select element is rendered as ONE labelled point ("Spec01"..."Spec10").}
  \CommentTok{\# In this mode spec\_layout / spec\_relation / scale\_networks have no effect (no network drawn).}
  \CommentTok{\# The point positions are decided by \textasciigrave{}group\_layout\textasciigrave{} below.}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_collapse =}\NormalTok{ T,}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Env blocks (the four heatmap quadrants) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Each element defines one env block; each block becomes one heatmap quadrant.}
  \AttributeTok{env\_select =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Env01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{14}\NormalTok{,}
                    \AttributeTok{Env02 =} \DecValTok{15}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{28}\NormalTok{,}
                    \AttributeTok{Env03 =} \DecValTok{29}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{42}\NormalTok{,}
                    \AttributeTok{Env04 =} \DecValTok{43}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{56}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Orientation of the four heatmaps {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Order pairs with env\_select: Env01 {-}\textgreater{} top\_right, Env02 {-}\textgreater{} bottom\_right, etc.}
  \AttributeTok{orientation =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"top\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
                  \StringTok{"bottom\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
                  \StringTok{"top\_left"}\NormalTok{,}
                  \StringTok{"bottom\_left"}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# spec\_layout = c("rectangle\_outline", "square\_outline", "circle\_outline"),}
  \CommentTok{\# \^{} Node{-}shape layout for the central species networks; ignored when spec\_collapse = T,}
  \CommentTok{\#   so commenting it out makes no difference here.}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Relation method (this run uses mantel) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{relation\_method =} \StringTok{"mantel"}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# use the mantel branch instead of correlation}
  \AttributeTok{mantel.method   =} \StringTok{"mantel"}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# legacy compatibility argument; unused by the current implementation, leave as{-}is}
  \AttributeTok{mantel\_kind     =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"col\_vs\_col"}\NormalTok{),         }\CommentTok{\# * mantel granularity:}
                                             \CommentTok{\#   "col\_vs\_col"   = legacy mode: spec single{-}column dist}
                                             \CommentTok{\#                    vs env single{-}column dist (close to rank correlation)}
                                             \CommentTok{\#   "block\_vs\_col" = recommended ecological mode (default):}
                                             \CommentTok{\#                    spec WHOLE{-}BLOCK dist vs single env column dist}
                                             \CommentTok{\# }\AlertTok{NOTE}\CommentTok{: under col\_vs\_col + spec\_collapse = T, every link generated}
                                             \CommentTok{\#       by each spec column inside a block starts from the SAME}
                                             \CommentTok{\#       collapsed point — a console message will warn about this.}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# correlation method; under mantel mode it falls through to mantel.method2; also used for env{-}env autocorrelation heatmaps}
  \AttributeTok{cor.use    =} \StringTok{"pairwise"}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# NA handling: pairwise{-}complete observations}
  \AttributeTok{drop\_nonsig =}\NormalTok{ T,                           }\CommentTok{\# T = hide non{-}significant links from the plot}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Central{-}network geometry {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{r =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,                                     }\CommentTok{\# base radius of the central network; under spec\_collapse = T only controls how tightly the points pack}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_networks =}\NormalTok{ F,                        }\CommentTok{\# F = do NOT normalise all networks to a common size;}
                                             \CommentTok{\#     auto{-}disabled when spec\_collapse = T}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Overall arrangement of the (collapsed) spec points {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{group\_layout =} \StringTok{"circle"}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# ring layout: the 10 points are spread evenly around a circle}
  \CommentTok{\# nrow = 5,                                \# only used by row/column/snake layouts; no effect for circle}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist =} \DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{,                           }\CommentTok{\# distance from each point to the centre (radius of the ring)}
  \AttributeTok{distance    =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                           }\CommentTok{\# offset between the centre point group and the outer heatmaps}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Heatmap text annotations {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelSize  =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                     }\CommentTok{\# font size for heatmap row/col labels (ID/Type)}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapSigSize    =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,                     }\CommentTok{\# font size for the significance marks (*, **, ***) inside heatmap tiles}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Per{-}quadrant colour palettes (low {-}\textgreater{} high) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Length must match \textasciigrave{}orientation\textasciigrave{} (4 quadrants); paired in order.}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapColorBar =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 1: top\_right}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 2: bottom\_right}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#4393c3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#d6604d"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 3: top\_left}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#92c5de"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#f4a582"}\NormalTok{)                  }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 4: bottom\_left}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelOrient =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# rotate heatmap labels 45 deg to avoid overlap}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Spec{-}env link (segment) styling {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineWidth =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# min / max line width for significant links (mapped by {-}log10(p))}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineColor =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#9ecae1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{),    }\CommentTok{\# colour gradient endpoints for mantel r low / high}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Restrict which (env\_block, spec\_block) pairs are drawn {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Under mantel mode, each (env\_block, spec\_block) pair triggers a set of mantel tests}
  \CommentTok{\# (col\_vs\_col: |spec cols| x |env cols| tests; block\_vs\_col: |env cols| tests).}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons =}\NormalTok{ T,                           }\CommentTok{\# enable the restriction (otherwise ALL possible pairs are drawn)}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons\_groups =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env01"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec01"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env01 paired only with Spec01}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env02"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec02"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env02 paired only with Spec02}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env03"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec03"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env03 paired only with Spec03}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env04"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec03"}\NormalTok{)                     }\CommentTok{\# Env04 also paired only with Spec03}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Point sizes {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointSize =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                      }\CommentTok{\# size of the anchor points on each heatmap\textquotesingle{}s diagonal}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointSize    =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,                     }\CommentTok{\# size of the collapsed spec points (larger than non{-}collapse default for visibility)}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Point fill colours (vectors are recycled in order) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# HeatmapPointFill: length = \# of quadrants (4), paired with \textasciigrave{}orientation\textasciigrave{} in order.}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointFill =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# CorePointFill: length = \# of spec\_select blocks (10), paired with names(spec\_select) in order.}
  \CommentTok{\# With spec\_collapse = T, each block is one point, so 1 colour = 1 point.}
  \CommentTok{\# First 5 points blue, last 5 points red — visually splits the 10 blocks into two groups.}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointFill =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{,}
                    \StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## `spec_collapse = TRUE` collapses each spec block into one point; in the current mode (relation_method = 'mantel', mantel_kind = 'col_vs_col') link sources are per spec column, so all column-level links from a block will originate from the same collapsed point. Consider `relation_method = 'mantel'` + `mantel_kind = 'block_vs_col'` for a one-line-per-block visualisation.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{p2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gglink\_heatmaps}\NormalTok{(}
  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Data input {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{env                =}\NormalTok{ Envdf\_4st,            }\CommentTok{\# environmental data frame: one column per env variable}
  \AttributeTok{spec               =}\NormalTok{ Spedf,                }\CommentTok{\# species data frame: one column per species / taxonomic unit}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Spec blocks (central networks) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Each list element defines one central species network (column indices into \textasciigrave{}spec\textasciigrave{}).}
  \CommentTok{\# The block names (Spec01, Spec02 ...) are used later to match \textasciigrave{}comparisons\_groups\textasciigrave{}.}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_select        =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Spec01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec02 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{15}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec03 =} \DecValTok{16}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec04 =} \DecValTok{16}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec05 =} \DecValTok{16}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec06 =} \DecValTok{16}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec07 =} \DecValTok{5}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec08 =} \DecValTok{21}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{25}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec09 =} \DecValTok{26}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{35}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec10 =} \DecValTok{36}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{44}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Env blocks (the four heatmap quadrants) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Each element defines one env block; each block becomes one heatmap quadrant.}
  \AttributeTok{env\_select         =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Env01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{14}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Env02 =} \DecValTok{15}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{28}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Env03 =} \DecValTok{29}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{42}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Env04 =} \DecValTok{43}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{56}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Orientation of the four heatmaps {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Order pairs with env\_select: Env01 {-}\textgreater{} top\_right, Env02 {-}\textgreater{} bottom\_right, etc.}
  \AttributeTok{orientation        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"top\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"bottom\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"top\_left"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"bottom\_left"}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# spec\_layout      = c("rectangle\_outline", "square\_outline", "circle\_outline"),}
  \CommentTok{\# \^{} Node{-}shape layout for the central species networks; commented out here, so the default "circle\_outline" is used.}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Correlation parameters {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method         =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# correlation method: pearson / kendall / spearman}
  \AttributeTok{cor.use            =} \StringTok{"pairwise"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# NA handling: pairwise{-}complete observations}
  \AttributeTok{drop\_nonsig        =}\NormalTok{ T,                    }\CommentTok{\# T = hide non{-}significant links from the plot}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Central{-}network geometry {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{r                  =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# base radius of the central network}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_networks     =}\NormalTok{ F,                    }\CommentTok{\# F = do NOT normalise all networks to a common size;}
                                             \CommentTok{\#     networks with more nodes will be scaled up proportionally}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Overall arrangement of the multiple spec networks {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{group\_layout       =} \StringTok{"snake"}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# snake layout (rows zig{-}zag back and forth)}
  \AttributeTok{nrow               =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# number of rows used by the snake layout (10 networks {-}\textgreater{} 5 rows x 2 cols)}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist        =} \DecValTok{8}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# spacing between adjacent central networks}
  \AttributeTok{distance           =} \DecValTok{6}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# offset between the central networks and the outer heatmaps}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Heatmap text annotations {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelSize   =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# font size for heatmap row/col labels (ID/Type)}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapSigSize     =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# font size for the significance marks (*, **, ***) inside heatmap tiles}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Per{-}quadrant colour palettes (low {-}\textgreater{} high) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Length must match \textasciigrave{}orientation\textasciigrave{} (4 quadrants); paired in order.}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapColorBar    =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 1: top\_right}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 2: bottom\_right}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#4393c3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#d6604d"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 3: top\_left}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#92c5de"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#f4a582"}\NormalTok{)                  }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 4: bottom\_left}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelOrient =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# rotate heatmap labels 45 deg to avoid overlap}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Spec{-}env link (segment) styling {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineWidth       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{),              }\CommentTok{\# min / max line width for the links (mapped by {-}log10(p))}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineColor       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#9ecae1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# colour gradient endpoints for correlation low / high}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Restrict which (env\_block, spec\_block) pairs are drawn {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons        =}\NormalTok{ T,                    }\CommentTok{\# enable the restriction (otherwise ALL possible pairs are drawn)}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons\_groups =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env01"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec01"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env01 paired only with Spec01}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env02"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec02"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env02 paired only with Spec02}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env03"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec03"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env03 paired only with Spec03}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env04"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec03"}\NormalTok{)                     }\CommentTok{\# Env04 also paired only with Spec03}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Point sizes {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointSize   =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# size of the anchor points on each heatmap\textquotesingle{}s diagonal}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointSize      =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# size of the central{-}network nodes}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Point fill colours (vectors are recycled in order) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# HeatmapPointFill: length = \# of quadrants (4), paired with \textasciigrave{}orientation\textasciigrave{} in order.}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointFill   =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# CorePointFill: length = \# of spec\_select blocks (10), paired with names(spec\_select) in order.}
  \CommentTok{\# First 5 networks coloured blue, last 5 coloured red — visually splits the 10 networks into two groups.}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointFill      =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}

\section{Network Environment (combine-core to one point)}\label{network-environment-combine-core-to-one-point}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{p2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gglink\_heatmaps}\NormalTok{(}
  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Data input {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{env                =}\NormalTok{ Envdf\_4st,            }\CommentTok{\# environmental data frame: one column per env variable}
  \AttributeTok{spec               =}\NormalTok{ Spedf,                }\CommentTok{\# species data frame: one column per species / taxonomic unit}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Spec blocks (central networks) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Each list element defines one central species network (column indices into \textasciigrave{}spec\textasciigrave{}).}
  \CommentTok{\# The block names (Spec01, Spec02 ...) are used later to match \textasciigrave{}comparisons\_groups\textasciigrave{}.}
  \CommentTok{\# }\AlertTok{NOTE}\CommentTok{: spec\_collapse = TRUE is enabled below, so these 6 blocks are NOT drawn as}
  \CommentTok{\#       networks — each one is collapsed into a single labelled point bearing the block name.}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_select        =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Spec01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec02 =} \DecValTok{11}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec03 =} \DecValTok{5}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{21}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec04 =} \DecValTok{20}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{26}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec05 =} \DecValTok{30}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{40}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec06 =} \DecValTok{25}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{44}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Collapse each spec\_block into a single point {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# T = each spec\_select element is rendered as ONE labelled point ("Spec01"..."Spec06").}
  \CommentTok{\# In this mode spec\_layout / spec\_relation / scale\_networks have no effect (no network drawn).}
  \CommentTok{\# The point positions are decided by \textasciigrave{}group\_layout\textasciigrave{} below.}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_collapse      =}\NormalTok{ T,}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Env blocks (the four heatmap quadrants) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Each element defines one env block; each block becomes one heatmap quadrant.}
  \AttributeTok{env\_select         =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Env01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{14}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Env02 =} \DecValTok{15}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{28}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Env03 =} \DecValTok{29}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{42}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Env04 =} \DecValTok{43}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{56}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Orientation of the four heatmaps {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Order pairs with env\_select: Env01 {-}\textgreater{} top\_right, Env02 {-}\textgreater{} bottom\_right, etc.}
  \AttributeTok{orientation        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"top\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"bottom\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"top\_left"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"bottom\_left"}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# spec\_layout      = c("rectangle\_outline", "square\_outline", "circle\_outline"),}
  \CommentTok{\# \^{} Node{-}shape layout for the central species networks; ignored when spec\_collapse = T,}
  \CommentTok{\#   so commenting it out makes no difference here.}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Relation method (this run uses mantel) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{relation\_method    =} \StringTok{"mantel"}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# use the mantel branch instead of correlation}
  \AttributeTok{mantel.method      =} \StringTok{"mantel"}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# legacy compatibility argument; unused by the current implementation, leave as{-}is}
  \AttributeTok{mantel\_kind        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"col\_vs\_col"}\NormalTok{),      }\CommentTok{\# * mantel granularity:}
                                             \CommentTok{\#   "col\_vs\_col"   = legacy mode: spec single{-}column dist}
                                             \CommentTok{\#                    vs env single{-}column dist (close to rank correlation)}
                                             \CommentTok{\#   "block\_vs\_col" = recommended ecological mode (default):}
                                             \CommentTok{\#                    spec WHOLE{-}BLOCK dist vs single env column dist}
                                             \CommentTok{\# }\AlertTok{NOTE}\CommentTok{: under col\_vs\_col + spec\_collapse = T, every link generated}
                                             \CommentTok{\#       by each spec column inside a block starts from the SAME}
                                             \CommentTok{\#       collapsed point — a console message will warn about this.}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method         =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# correlation method; under mantel mode it falls through to mantel.method2; also used for env{-}env autocorrelation heatmaps}
  \AttributeTok{cor.use            =} \StringTok{"pairwise"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# NA handling: pairwise{-}complete observations}
  \AttributeTok{drop\_nonsig        =}\NormalTok{ T,                    }\CommentTok{\# T = hide non{-}significant links from the plot}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Central{-}network geometry {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{r                  =} \FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{,                  }\CommentTok{\# base radius of the central network; under spec\_collapse = T only controls how tightly the points pack}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_networks     =}\NormalTok{ F,                    }\CommentTok{\# F = do NOT normalise all networks to a common size;}
                                             \CommentTok{\#     auto{-}disabled when spec\_collapse = T}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Overall arrangement of the (collapsed) spec points {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{group\_layout       =} \StringTok{"row"}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# row layout: points are placed along a horizontal row}
  \AttributeTok{group\_angle        =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# rotate the whole point row by 45 deg around the centre}
  \CommentTok{\# nrow             = 5,                    \# only used by row/column/snake when wrapping into multiple rows; not needed here}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist        =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# spacing between adjacent points along the row}
  \AttributeTok{distance           =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# offset between the centre point group and the outer heatmaps (negative {-}\textgreater{} pull heatmaps INWARD, toward the centre)}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Heatmap text annotations {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelSize   =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# font size for heatmap row/col labels (ID/Type)}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapSigSize     =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# font size for the significance marks (*, **, ***) inside heatmap tiles}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Per{-}quadrant colour palettes (low {-}\textgreater{} high) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Length must match \textasciigrave{}orientation\textasciigrave{} (4 quadrants); paired in order.}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapColorBar    =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 1: top\_right}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 2: bottom\_right}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#4393c3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#d6604d"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 3: top\_left}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#92c5de"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#f4a582"}\NormalTok{)                  }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 4: bottom\_left}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelOrient =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# rotate heatmap labels 45 deg to avoid overlap}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Spec{-}env link (segment) styling {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineWidth       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{),              }\CommentTok{\# min / max line width for the links (mapped by {-}log10(p))}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineColor       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#9ecae1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# colour gradient endpoints for mantel r low / high}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Restrict which (env\_block, spec\_block) pairs are drawn {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Under mantel mode, each (env\_block, spec\_block) pair triggers a set of mantel tests}
  \CommentTok{\# (col\_vs\_col: |spec cols| x |env cols| tests; block\_vs\_col: |env cols| tests).}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons        =}\NormalTok{ T,                    }\CommentTok{\# enable the restriction (otherwise ALL possible pairs are drawn)}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons\_groups =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env01"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec01"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env01 paired only with Spec01}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env02"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec02"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env02 paired only with Spec02}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env03"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec03"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env03 paired only with Spec03}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env04"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec03"}\NormalTok{)                     }\CommentTok{\# Env04 also paired only with Spec03}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Point sizes {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointSize   =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# size of the anchor points on each heatmap\textquotesingle{}s diagonal}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointSize      =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# size of the collapsed spec points (larger than non{-}collapse default for visibility)}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Point fill colours (vectors are recycled in order) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# HeatmapPointFill: length = \# of quadrants (4), paired with \textasciigrave{}orientation\textasciigrave{} in order.}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointFill   =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# CorePointFill: length = \# of spec\_select blocks (6), paired with names(spec\_select) in order.}
  \CommentTok{\# With spec\_collapse = T, each block is one point, so 1 colour = 1 point.}
  \CommentTok{\# Six distinct colours here, one per Spec01..Spec06 — visually distinguishes every block.}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointFill      =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#9ecae1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## `spec_collapse = TRUE` collapses each spec block into one point; in the current mode (relation_method = 'mantel', mantel_kind = 'col_vs_col') link sources are per spec column, so all column-level links from a block will originate from the same collapsed point. Consider `relation_method = 'mantel'` + `mantel_kind = 'block_vs_col'` for a one-line-per-block visualisation.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{verbatim}
## `distance = -3` is negative; the env heatmaps will be pulled inward and may overlap the central point group. If a heatmap anchor becomes <= 0 you will get a warning and the corresponding heatmap may flip to the opposite side -- use a less negative value if that happens.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{Shaded}

\section{Change group angle}\label{change-group-angle}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\NormalTok{p3 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{gglink\_heatmaps}\NormalTok{(}
  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Data input {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{env                =}\NormalTok{ Envdf\_4st,            }\CommentTok{\# environmental data frame: one column per env variable}
  \AttributeTok{spec               =}\NormalTok{ Spedf,                }\CommentTok{\# species data frame: one column per species / taxonomic unit}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Spec blocks (central networks) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Each list element defines one central species network (column indices into \textasciigrave{}spec\textasciigrave{}).}
  \CommentTok{\# The block names (Spec01, Spec02 ...) are used later to match \textasciigrave{}comparisons\_groups\textasciigrave{}.}
  \CommentTok{\# }\AlertTok{NOTE}\CommentTok{: spec\_collapse = TRUE is enabled below, so these 6 blocks are NOT drawn as}
  \CommentTok{\#       networks — each one is collapsed into a single labelled point bearing the block name.}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_select        =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Spec01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec02 =} \DecValTok{11}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{20}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec03 =} \DecValTok{5}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{21}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec04 =} \DecValTok{20}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{26}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec05 =} \DecValTok{30}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{40}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Spec06 =} \DecValTok{25}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{44}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Collapse each spec\_block into a single point {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# T = each spec\_select element is rendered as ONE labelled point ("Spec01"..."Spec06").}
  \CommentTok{\# In this mode spec\_layout / spec\_relation / scale\_networks have no effect (no network drawn).}
  \CommentTok{\# The point positions are decided by \textasciigrave{}group\_layout\textasciigrave{} below.}
  \AttributeTok{spec\_collapse      =}\NormalTok{ T,}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Env blocks (the four heatmap quadrants) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Each element defines one env block; each block becomes one heatmap quadrant.}
  \AttributeTok{env\_select         =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{Env01 =} \DecValTok{1}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{14}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Env02 =} \DecValTok{15}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{28}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Env03 =} \DecValTok{29}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{42}\NormalTok{,}
                            \AttributeTok{Env04 =} \DecValTok{43}\SpecialCharTok{:}\DecValTok{56}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Orientation of the four heatmaps {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Order pairs with env\_select: Env01 {-}\textgreater{} top\_right, Env02 {-}\textgreater{} bottom\_right, etc.}
  \AttributeTok{orientation        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"top\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"bottom\_right"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"top\_left"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"bottom\_left"}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# spec\_layout      = c("rectangle\_outline", "square\_outline", "circle\_outline"),}
  \CommentTok{\# \^{} Node{-}shape layout for the central species networks; ignored when spec\_collapse = T,}
  \CommentTok{\#   so commenting it out makes no difference here.}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Relation method (this run uses mantel) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{relation\_method    =} \StringTok{"mantel"}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# use the mantel branch instead of correlation}
  \AttributeTok{mantel.method      =} \StringTok{"mantel"}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# legacy compatibility argument; unused by the current implementation, leave as{-}is}
  \AttributeTok{mantel\_kind        =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"col\_vs\_col"}\NormalTok{),      }\CommentTok{\# * mantel granularity:}
                                             \CommentTok{\#   "col\_vs\_col"   = legacy mode: spec single{-}column dist}
                                             \CommentTok{\#                    vs env single{-}column dist (close to rank correlation)}
                                             \CommentTok{\#   "block\_vs\_col" = recommended ecological mode (default):}
                                             \CommentTok{\#                    spec WHOLE{-}BLOCK dist vs single env column dist}
                                             \CommentTok{\# }\AlertTok{NOTE}\CommentTok{: under col\_vs\_col + spec\_collapse = T, every link generated}
                                             \CommentTok{\#       by each spec column inside a block starts from the SAME}
                                             \CommentTok{\#       collapsed point — a console message will warn about this.}
  \AttributeTok{cor.method         =} \StringTok{"pearson"}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# correlation method; under mantel mode it falls through to mantel.method2; also used for env{-}env autocorrelation heatmaps}
  \AttributeTok{cor.use            =} \StringTok{"pairwise"}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# NA handling: pairwise{-}complete observations}
  \AttributeTok{drop\_nonsig        =}\NormalTok{ T,                    }\CommentTok{\# T = hide non{-}significant links from the plot}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Central{-}network geometry {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{r                  =} \FloatTok{0.1}\NormalTok{,                  }\CommentTok{\# base radius of the central network; under spec\_collapse = T only controls how tightly the points pack}
  \AttributeTok{scale\_networks     =}\NormalTok{ F,                    }\CommentTok{\# F = do NOT normalise all networks to a common size;}
                                             \CommentTok{\#     auto{-}disabled when spec\_collapse = T}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Overall arrangement of the (collapsed) spec points {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{group\_layout       =} \StringTok{"arc"}\NormalTok{,                }\CommentTok{\# arc layout: points are spread along an ARC (a partial circle, not a full ring)}
  \AttributeTok{group\_angle        =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# rotate the whole arc by 45 deg around the centre (starting angle)}
  \AttributeTok{group\_arc\_angle    =} \DecValTok{90}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# angular span of the arc in degrees: 90 = quarter circle, 180 = half circle, 360 = full ring}
  \CommentTok{\# nrow             = 5,                    \# only used by row/column/snake when wrapping into multiple rows; not needed here}
  \AttributeTok{anchor\_dist        =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# distance from each point to the centre (radius of the arc)}
  \AttributeTok{distance           =} \SpecialCharTok{{-}}\DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# offset between the centre point group and the outer heatmaps (negative {-}\textgreater{} pull heatmaps INWARD, toward the centre)}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Heatmap text annotations {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelSize   =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# font size for heatmap row/col labels (ID/Type)}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapSigSize     =} \DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# font size for the significance marks (*, **, ***) inside heatmap tiles}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Per{-}quadrant colour palettes (low {-}\textgreater{} high) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Length must match \textasciigrave{}orientation\textasciigrave{} (4 quadrants); paired in order.}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapColorBar    =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 1: top\_right}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 2: bottom\_right}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#4393c3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#d6604d"}\NormalTok{),                 }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 3: top\_left}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#92c5de"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#f4a582"}\NormalTok{)                  }\CommentTok{\# quadrant 4: bottom\_left}
\NormalTok{  ),}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapLabelOrient =} \DecValTok{45}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# rotate heatmap labels 45 deg to avoid overlap}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Spec{-}env link (segment) styling {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineWidth       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{),              }\CommentTok{\# min / max line width for the links (mapped by {-}log10(p))}
  \AttributeTok{SigLineColor       =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#9ecae1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{),  }\CommentTok{\# colour gradient endpoints for mantel r low / high}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Restrict which (env\_block, spec\_block) pairs are drawn {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# Under mantel mode, each (env\_block, spec\_block) pair triggers a set of mantel tests}
  \CommentTok{\# (col\_vs\_col: |spec cols| x |env cols| tests; block\_vs\_col: |env cols| tests).}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons        =}\NormalTok{ T,                    }\CommentTok{\# enable the restriction (otherwise ALL possible pairs are drawn)}
  \AttributeTok{comparisons\_groups =} \FunctionTok{list}\NormalTok{(}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env01"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec01"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env01 paired only with Spec01}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env02"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec02"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env02 paired only with Spec02}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env03"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec03"}\NormalTok{),                    }\CommentTok{\# Env03 paired only with Spec03}
    \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Env04"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"Spec03"}\NormalTok{)                     }\CommentTok{\# Env04 also paired only with Spec03}
\NormalTok{  ),}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Point sizes {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointSize   =} \DecValTok{4}\NormalTok{,                    }\CommentTok{\# size of the anchor points on each heatmap\textquotesingle{}s diagonal}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointSize      =} \DecValTok{10}\NormalTok{,                   }\CommentTok{\# size of the collapsed spec points (larger than non{-}collapse default for visibility)}

  \CommentTok{\# {-}{-}{-}{-} Point fill colours (vectors are recycled in order) {-}{-}{-}{-}}
  \CommentTok{\# HeatmapPointFill: length = \# of quadrants (4), paired with \textasciigrave{}orientation\textasciigrave{} in order.}
  \AttributeTok{HeatmapPointFill   =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{),}

  \CommentTok{\# CorePointFill: length = \# of spec\_select blocks (6), paired with names(spec\_select) in order.}
  \CommentTok{\# With spec\_collapse = T, each block is one point, so 1 colour = 1 point.}
  \CommentTok{\# Six distinct colours, one per Spec01..Spec06 — visually distinguishes every block.}
  \AttributeTok{CorePointFill      =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#2166ac"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2182b"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#1b7837"}\NormalTok{,}
                         \StringTok{"\#762a83"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#9ecae1"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fc9272"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{)}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{verbatim}
## `spec_collapse = TRUE` collapses each spec block into one point; in the current mode (relation_method = 'mantel', mantel_kind = 'col_vs_col') link sources are per spec column, so all column-level links from a block will originate from the same collapsed point. Consider `relation_method = 'mantel'` + `mantel_kind = 'block_vs_col'` for a one-line-per-block visualisation.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{verbatim}
## `distance = -3` is negative; the env heatmaps will be pulled inward and may overlap the central point group. If a heatmap anchor becomes <= 0 you will get a warning and the corresponding heatmap may flip to the opposite side -- use a less negative value if that happens.
\end{verbatim}

\begin{verbatim}
## Warning: `distance = -3` is too negative for the current central layout -- at
## least one heatmap anchor became <= 0. The corresponding heatmap will flip to
## the opposite side or collapse onto the origin. Try a larger (less negative)
## `distance`.
\end{verbatim}

Load R Package

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(tidyverse)   }\CommentTok{\# data manipulation / piping utilities}
\FunctionTok{library}\NormalTok{(ggNetView)   }\CommentTok{\# graph builders + info{-}extraction helpers}
\end{Highlighting}

\section{Microbial Community Network}\label{microbial-community-network}

\begin{quote}
The data from \texttt{Liu,\ X.,\ Wang,\ M.,\ Liu,\ B.\ et\ al.\ Keystone\ taxa\ mediate\ the\ trade-off\ between\ microbial\ community\ stability\ and\ performance\ in\ activated\ sludges.\ Nat\ Water\ 3,\ 723–733\ (2025).} \href{https://doi.org/10.1038/s44221-025-00451-6}{DOI:10.1038/s44221-025-00451-6}
\end{quote}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# Load data}

\CommentTok{\# OTU table: read the pre{-}filtered ASV abundance data (relative abundance range 0.00001{-}0.01)}
\CommentTok{\# Use read\_delim() to read the CSV file, and convert the first column ("...1") to row names}
\NormalTok{otu }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_delim}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{file =} \StringTok{"./Input/Figure3a{-}b\_ASV\_data\_0.00001\_0.01.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{delim =} \StringTok{","}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{col\_names =}\NormalTok{ T) }\SpecialCharTok{\%\textgreater{}\%}
\NormalTok{  tibble}\SpecialCharTok{::}\FunctionTok{column\_to\_rownames}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{var =} \StringTok{"...1"}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# OTU taxonomy table: read the ASV annotation information from the MiDAS database,}
\CommentTok{\# including taxonomy, Mean Relative Abundance (MRA), and functional annotations.}
\CommentTok{\# This table will be used to annotate the network nodes (e.g. by Phylum).}
\NormalTok{tax }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{read\_delim}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{file =} \StringTok{"./Input/Figure3a{-}b\_ASV{-}MiDAS{-}MRA{-}function{-}taxonomy{-}all{-}new.csv"}\NormalTok{, }\AttributeTok{col\_names =}\NormalTok{ T, }\AttributeTok{delim =} \StringTok{","}\NormalTok{)}

\CommentTok{\# Build the network graph object}
\CommentTok{\# build\_graph\_from\_mat() computes pairwise correlations among taxa from the OTU abundance matrix}
\CommentTok{\# and constructs a network graph object for downstream visualization.}
\NormalTok{obj }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{build\_graph\_from\_mat}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{mat              =}\NormalTok{ otu,        }\CommentTok{\# input OTU abundance matrix}
  \AttributeTok{transfrom.method =} \StringTok{"ln"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# data transformation method: natural log (stabilize variance)}
  \AttributeTok{method           =} \StringTok{"WGCNA"}\NormalTok{,    }\CommentTok{\# correlation method: WGCNA (weighted gene co{-}expression network)}
  \AttributeTok{r.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.44}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# correlation coefficient threshold: keep edges with |r| \textgreater{}= 0.44}
  \AttributeTok{p.threshold      =} \FloatTok{0.05}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# significance threshold: keep edges with p \textless{} 0.05}
  \AttributeTok{node\_annotation  =}\NormalTok{ tax         }\CommentTok{\# node annotation table providing taxonomic info for each node}
\NormalTok{)}


\CommentTok{\# ====== Figure 1: network plot colored by Modularity (module assignment) ======}
\NormalTok{p1 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{graph\_obj     =}\NormalTok{ obj,           }\CommentTok{\# input network graph object}
  \AttributeTok{layout        =} \StringTok{"gephi"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# layout algorithm: Gephi{-}style (ForceAtlas2{-}like)}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module =} \StringTok{"adjacent"}\NormalTok{,    }\CommentTok{\# module layout: place adjacent modules close together}
  \AttributeTok{group.by      =} \StringTok{"Modularity"}\NormalTok{,  }\CommentTok{\# grouping variable: color nodes by module ID}
  \AttributeTok{center        =}\NormalTok{ F,             }\CommentTok{\# do not center the network}
  \AttributeTok{jitter        =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,          }\CommentTok{\# add small random offsets to node positions to avoid overlap}
  \AttributeTok{jitter\_sd     =} \FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{,          }\CommentTok{\# standard deviation of the jitter}
  \AttributeTok{shrink        =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# shrink factor: tighten nodes within each module}
  \AttributeTok{linealpha     =} \FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# transparency of edges}
  \AttributeTok{linecolor     =} \StringTok{"\#d9d9d9"}      \CommentTok{\# default edge color: light grey}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} 
  \CommentTok{\# Manually assign fill colors for the 16 modules}
  \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#00A9FF"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#0BB702"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#C77CFF"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00C19A"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#ED68ED"}\NormalTok{, }
                               \StringTok{"\#8494FF"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00B8E7"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#FF61CC"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#F8766D"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#E68613"}\NormalTok{,}
                               \StringTok{"\#ABA300"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#7CAE00"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#CD9600"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00BE67"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00BFC4"}\NormalTok{,}
                               \StringTok{"\#FF68A1"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+} 
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{)    }\CommentTok{\# remove legend}

\CommentTok{\# Save Figure 1 as a PDF (commented out; uncomment to enable saving)}
\CommentTok{\# ggsave(filename = "Figure3a{-}b/Figure3a.pdf",}
\CommentTok{\#        plot     = p1,}
\CommentTok{\#        height   = 12,}
\CommentTok{\#        width    = 12)}

\NormalTok{p1   }\CommentTok{\# display Figure 1}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{center}\includegraphics{ggNetView-manual_files/figure-latex/unnamed-chunk-135-1} \end{center}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# ====== Figure 2: add  outlier ======}

\NormalTok{module\_colors }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#00A9FF"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#0BB702"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#C77CFF"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00C19A"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#ED68ED"}\NormalTok{, }
                   \StringTok{"\#8494FF"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00B8E7"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#FF61CC"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#F8766D"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#E68613"}\NormalTok{,}
                   \StringTok{"\#ABA300"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#7CAE00"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#CD9600"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00BE67"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00BFC4"}\NormalTok{,}
                   \StringTok{"\#FF68A1"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{p2 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{graph\_obj        =}\NormalTok{ obj,             }\CommentTok{\# input network graph object}
  \AttributeTok{layout           =} \StringTok{"gephi"}\NormalTok{,         }\CommentTok{\# layout algorithm: Gephi{-}style (ForceAtlas2{-}like)}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module    =} \StringTok{"adjacent"}\NormalTok{,      }\CommentTok{\# module layout: place adjacent modules close together}
  \AttributeTok{group.by         =} \StringTok{"Modularity"}\NormalTok{,    }\CommentTok{\# grouping variable: color nodes by module ID}
  \AttributeTok{center           =}\NormalTok{ F,               }\CommentTok{\# do not force a node at the layout centre}
  \AttributeTok{pointalpha       =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# node transparency (0 = invisible, 1 = opaque)}
  \AttributeTok{label            =}\NormalTok{ T,               }\CommentTok{\# draw module text labels}
  \AttributeTok{label\_layout     =} \StringTok{"two\_column"}\NormalTok{,    }\CommentTok{\# label\_layout defaults to "two\_column": labels are placed in two fixed}
  \AttributeTok{label\_wrap\_width =} \DecValTok{18}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# wrap long module / pathway names at \textasciitilde{}18 chars per line}
  \AttributeTok{label\_outer\_pad  =} \FloatTok{0.25}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# distance from the network edge to each label column}
  \AttributeTok{jitter           =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# add small random offsets to node positions}
  \AttributeTok{jitter\_sd        =} \FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# standard deviation of the jitter}
  \AttributeTok{shrink           =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# shrink factor: tighten nodes within each module}
  \AttributeTok{linealpha        =} \FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# transparency of edges}
  \AttributeTok{linecolor        =} \StringTok{"\#d9d9d9"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# default edge colour: light grey}
  \AttributeTok{add\_outer        =}\NormalTok{ T,               }\CommentTok{\# add per{-}module outer boundary (KDE/HDR contour)}
  \AttributeTok{outeralpha       =} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# per{-}module boundary fill transparency}
  \AttributeTok{outerwidth       =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# per{-}module boundary line width}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer  =}\NormalTok{ T,               }\CommentTok{\# add an outer circle around the whole network}
  \AttributeTok{fill             =}\NormalTok{ module\_colors,   }\CommentTok{\# \textless{}{-}{-} node fill colours; module outers use the same palette}
  \AttributeTok{color            =}\NormalTok{ module\_colors    }\CommentTok{\# \textless{}{-}{-} module boundary line colours (matches the fills)}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{)     }\CommentTok{\# hide all legends}


\CommentTok{\# Save Figure 2 as a PDF (commented out; uncomment to enable saving)}
\CommentTok{\# ggsave(filename = "Figure2.pdf",}
\CommentTok{\#        plot     = p2,}
\CommentTok{\#        height   = 12,}
\CommentTok{\#        width    = 12)}

\NormalTok{p2   }\CommentTok{\# display Figure 2}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# ====== Figure 3: add  outlier ======}

\NormalTok{module\_colors }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#00A9FF"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#0BB702"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#C77CFF"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00C19A"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#ED68ED"}\NormalTok{, }
                   \StringTok{"\#8494FF"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00B8E7"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#FF61CC"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#F8766D"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#E68613"}\NormalTok{,}
                   \StringTok{"\#ABA300"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#7CAE00"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#CD9600"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00BE67"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#00BFC4"}\NormalTok{,}
                   \StringTok{"\#FF68A1"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{p3 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{graph\_obj        =}\NormalTok{ obj,             }\CommentTok{\# input network graph object}
  \AttributeTok{layout           =} \StringTok{"gephi"}\NormalTok{,         }\CommentTok{\# layout algorithm: Gephi{-}style (ForceAtlas2{-}like)}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module    =} \StringTok{"adjacent"}\NormalTok{,      }\CommentTok{\# module layout: place adjacent modules close together}
  \AttributeTok{group.by         =} \StringTok{"Modularity"}\NormalTok{,    }\CommentTok{\# grouping variable: color nodes by module ID}
  \AttributeTok{center           =}\NormalTok{ F,               }\CommentTok{\# do not force a node at the layout centre}
  \AttributeTok{pointalpha       =} \FloatTok{0.5}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# node transparency (0 = invisible, 1 = opaque)}
  \AttributeTok{label            =}\NormalTok{ T,               }\CommentTok{\# draw module text labels}
  \AttributeTok{label\_layout     =} \StringTok{"two\_column\_follow"}\NormalTok{, }\CommentTok{\# "two\_column\_follow": ring layout that follows each module\textquotesingle{}s real}
  \AttributeTok{label\_wrap\_width =} \DecValTok{18}\NormalTok{,              }\CommentTok{\# wrap long module / pathway names at \textasciitilde{}18 chars per line}
  \AttributeTok{label\_outer\_pad  =} \FloatTok{0.25}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# outer ellipse padding: how far labels sit beyond the network}
  \AttributeTok{jitter           =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# add small random offsets to node positions}
  \AttributeTok{jitter\_sd        =} \FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# standard deviation of the jitter}
  \AttributeTok{shrink           =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# shrink factor: tighten nodes within each module}
  \AttributeTok{linealpha        =} \FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# transparency of edges}
  \AttributeTok{linecolor        =} \StringTok{"\#d9d9d9"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# default edge colour: light grey}
  \AttributeTok{add\_outer        =}\NormalTok{ T,               }\CommentTok{\# add per{-}module outer boundary (KDE/HDR contour)}
  \AttributeTok{bandwidth\_scale  =} \FloatTok{2.0}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# KDE bandwidth multiplier; \textgreater{}1 {-}\textgreater{} smoother / rounder boundary}
  \AttributeTok{outeralpha       =} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# per{-}module boundary fill transparency}
  \AttributeTok{outerwidth       =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# per{-}module boundary line width}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer  =}\NormalTok{ T,               }\CommentTok{\# add an outer circle around the whole network}
  \AttributeTok{fill             =}\NormalTok{ module\_colors,   }\CommentTok{\# \textless{}{-}{-} node fill colours; module outers use the same palette}
  \AttributeTok{color            =}\NormalTok{ module\_colors,   }\CommentTok{\# \textless{}{-}{-} module boundary line colours (matches the fills)}
  \AttributeTok{remove           =}\NormalTok{ T,               }\CommentTok{\# drop the "Others" module from rendering (keep layout intact)}
  \AttributeTok{mapping\_line     =}\NormalTok{ T                }\CommentTok{\# colour edges by their sign / weight category}
                                      \CommentTok{\# (e.g. positive vs negative correlations get different colours)}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{)     }\CommentTok{\# hide all legends}


\CommentTok{\# Save Figure 3 as a PDF (commented out; uncomment to enable saving)}
\CommentTok{\# ggsave(filename = "Figure3.pdf",}
\CommentTok{\#        plot     = p3,}
\CommentTok{\#        height   = 12,}
\CommentTok{\#        width    = 12)}

\NormalTok{p3   }\CommentTok{\# display Figure 3}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}

  \AttributeTok{graph\_obj        =}\NormalTok{ obj,             }\CommentTok{\# input network graph object}

  \AttributeTok{jitter\_sd        =} \FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{,            }\CommentTok{\# standard deviation of the jitter}
  \AttributeTok{shrink           =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# shrink factor: tighten nodes within each module}
  \AttributeTok{linealpha        =} \FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# transparency of edges}
  \AttributeTok{linecolor        =} \StringTok{"\#d9d9d9"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# default edge colour: light grey}
  \AttributeTok{add\_outer        =}\NormalTok{ T,               }\CommentTok{\# add per{-}module outer boundary (KDE/HDR contour)}
  \AttributeTok{outeralpha       =} \FloatTok{0.3}\NormalTok{,             }\CommentTok{\# per{-}module boundary fill transparency}
  \AttributeTok{outerwidth       =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{,               }\CommentTok{\# per{-}module boundary line width}
  \AttributeTok{add\_group\_outer  =}\NormalTok{ T,               }\CommentTok{\# add an outer circle around the whole network}
  \AttributeTok{fill             =}\NormalTok{ module\_colors,   }\CommentTok{\# \textless{}{-}{-} node fill colours; module outers use the same palette}
  \AttributeTok{color            =}\NormalTok{ module\_colors    }\CommentTok{\# \textless{}{-}{-} module boundary line colours (matches the fills)}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\CommentTok{\# ====== Figure 5: network plot colored by Phylum, with positive/negative edges mapped ======}
\NormalTok{p5 }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{ggNetView}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{graph\_obj     =}\NormalTok{ obj,           }\CommentTok{\# input the same network graph object}
  \AttributeTok{layout        =} \StringTok{"gephi"}\NormalTok{,       }\CommentTok{\# layout algorithm: Gephi{-}style}
  \AttributeTok{layout.module =} \StringTok{"adjacent"}\NormalTok{,    }\CommentTok{\# adjacent module layout}
  \AttributeTok{fill.by       =} \StringTok{"Phylum"}\NormalTok{,      }\CommentTok{\# grouping variable: color nodes by Phylum}
  \AttributeTok{center        =}\NormalTok{ F,             }\CommentTok{\# do not center the network}
  \AttributeTok{jitter        =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,          }\CommentTok{\# add jitter to node positions}
  \AttributeTok{jitter\_sd     =} \FloatTok{0.15}\NormalTok{,          }\CommentTok{\# jitter standard deviation}
  \AttributeTok{mapping\_line  =} \ConstantTok{TRUE}\NormalTok{,          }\CommentTok{\# map edge color by correlation sign (positive / negative)}
  \AttributeTok{shrink        =} \FloatTok{0.9}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# shrink factor for modules}
  \AttributeTok{linealpha     =} \FloatTok{0.2}\NormalTok{,           }\CommentTok{\# edge transparency}
  \AttributeTok{linecolor     =} \StringTok{"\#d9d9d9"}      \CommentTok{\# default edge color (overridden when mapping\_line = TRUE)}
\NormalTok{) }\SpecialCharTok{+} 
  \CommentTok{\# Manually assign fill colors for Phylum: a soft palette repeated 3 times to cover many phyla}
  \FunctionTok{scale\_fill\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =} \FunctionTok{rep}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"\#8dd3c7"}\NormalTok{, }
                                   \StringTok{"\#ffffb3"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#bebada"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fb8072"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#80b1d3"}\NormalTok{, }
                                   \StringTok{"\#fdb462"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b3de69"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fccde5"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#cab2d6"}\NormalTok{, }
                                   \StringTok{"\#bc80bd"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#ccebc5"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#ffed6f"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#a6cee3"}\NormalTok{, }
                                   \StringTok{"\#b2df8a"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fb9a99"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#bdbdbd"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#a6cee3"}\NormalTok{, }
                                   \StringTok{"\#1f78b4"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b2df8a"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#33a02c"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fb9a99"}\NormalTok{, }
                                   \StringTok{"\#e31a1c"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#fdbf6f"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#ff7f00"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#cab2d6"}\NormalTok{, }
                                   \StringTok{"\#6a3d9a"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#ffff99"}\NormalTok{, }\StringTok{"\#b15928"}\NormalTok{), }\AttributeTok{times =} \DecValTok{3}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+} 
  \CommentTok{\# Edge colors: red for positive correlations, blue for negative correlations}
  \FunctionTok{scale\_color\_manual}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{values =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\StringTok{"Positive"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#d6604d"}\NormalTok{,}
                                \StringTok{"Negative"} \OtherTok{=} \StringTok{"\#92c5de"}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+} 
  \FunctionTok{scale\_size}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{range =} \FunctionTok{c}\NormalTok{(}\DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{, }\DecValTok{5}\NormalTok{)) }\SpecialCharTok{+}      \CommentTok{\# map node size to the range [1, 5]}
  \FunctionTok{theme}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{legend.position =} \StringTok{"none"}\NormalTok{)    }\CommentTok{\# remove legend}

\CommentTok{\#        plot     = p5,}
\CommentTok{\#        height   = 12,}
\CommentTok{\#        width    = 12)}

\NormalTok{p5   }\CommentTok{\# display Figure 5}
\end{Highlighting}

\section{WGCNA network}\label{wgcna-network}

\begin{Shaded}
\begin{Highlighting}[]
\FunctionTok{load}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{file =} \StringTok{"./Input/WGCNA.tom{-}block.1.RData"}\NormalTok{)}
\NormalTok{TOM\_mat }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{as.matrix}\NormalTok{(TOM)}

\NormalTok{plot.mat }\OtherTok{=} \FunctionTok{readRDS}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{file =} \StringTok{"./Input/plot.mat.Rds"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{trans\_TOM }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{trans\_TOM\_in\_WGCNA}\NormalTok{(TOM\_mat, plot.mat, }\AttributeTok{top\_k =} \DecValTok{1}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{net }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{readRDS}\NormalTok{(}\AttributeTok{file =} \StringTok{"./Input/net.Rds"}\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{module }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{ID =} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(net}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{colors),}
  \AttributeTok{Module =} \FunctionTok{as.character}\NormalTok{(net}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{colors)}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{id }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{data.frame}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{ID =} \FunctionTok{names}\NormalTok{(}\FunctionTok{sort}\NormalTok{(net}\SpecialCharTok{$}\NormalTok{colors))}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{obj }\OtherTok{\textless{}{-}} \FunctionTok{build\_graph\_from\_wgcna}\NormalTok{(}
  \AttributeTok{wgcna\_tom =}\NormalTok{ trans\_TOM,}
  \AttributeTok{module =}\NormalTok{ module,}
  \AttributeTok{node\_annotation =}\NormalTok{ id}
\NormalTok{)}

\NormalTok{obj}
\end{Highlighting}
\end{Shaded}
